Summary

Lena asks Kara to be her fake date to Lex's wedding in an effort to get Lillian off her back and fend off any unwanted attention from her ex.
All Kara needs to do is pretend to be madly in love with Lena for a few weeks. A few practice kisses and a little acting between friends sounds easy enough.

    Chapter 1

    A light, but insistent, knocking on her front door woke Kara. She opened one eye to check her phone only to let out a groan when she saw it wasn’t even 6am. Knowing that nothing good would lead to someone knocking on her door at that time in the morning, she reluctantly rolled out of bed, not quite prepared for whatever drama was about to storm into her day. A night chasing a lead for an assignment had left her drained, her body not quite ready to leave the warmth of her duvet, but the knock came again, a little louder this time.

    Stepping through her apartment, she scraped her hair back loosely into a ponytail in a vain attempt to make it look like she’d not just woken up, even though the rest of her appearance and the ungodly hour were a dead giveaway. She pulled the door open a fraction just before a further series of knocks was about to be unleashed.

    “Lena?” Her head tilted in confusion as she took in the woman on her doorstep. Lena was everything Kara wasn’t in that moment. Dressed, made-up and amused.

    “Sorry, did we have plans?”

    “No, no. I just needed to see you,” she paused taking in Kara’s appearance. “I’m sorry, I forget that not everyone is up when I am, but this really couldn’t wait. I need to ask you something and it’s really time dependent. If I’d gone straight to work, I’d never get a chance to speak to you and…”

    “My mother was pushing me for an answer and I blurted out that I was seeing someone and your name was the first that came to mind, so?”

    “So?”

    “Will you?” 

    “Of course! You know I’d do anything for you. We’re best friends,” Kara offered a smile that she hoped would put her frazzled friends mind at ease. “Tell me everything, from the beginning and we can work from there.”

The pre-wedding family dinner that Lena’s ex-girlfriend, Andrea, was going to be at. The fact that her mother was always trying to push Lena to get back with Andrea even after what she’d done and that she’d chased her at 5am this morning to confirm who she was bringing with her.

Knowing no-one had ever really looked out for Lena when she was a child, was something that had always troubled Kara.

    “It was that bad?” Kara’s voice was laden with concern.

    “I wouldn’t be asking you to do this for me if it wasn’t.”

    “Come here,” Kara pulled Lena back into her, holding her for a little longer this time. “You get to work and then I’ll get to fixing your heart.”

    Lena stepped back with the softest smile on her face and Kara found herself lingering in her own doorway for a few moments after Lena had left. Her head full of ideas of how she could help, until she heard her alarm going off in her bedroom and realised she needed to get ready for work too.

Lena needed her. It really was that simple and she didn’t need Alex making more of a deal of it, when she was doing her best effort to convince herself it was all going to be fine.

When they rolled into the driveway Kara gasped, the Luthor mansion was even more imposing than Kara had imagined. Photos of it were one thing, but to be outside it was another. If there was any sign that Lena had been happy here, Kara wanted to know all about it, even if it was just a tale of how Lena used to hide in a corner of the library and get lost in the books there. Just to know that her whole life hadn’t been miserable and that once upon a time, Lena and Lex had laughed and played in the very bones of this house made Kara worry a little less about what Lena had been through and what lay ahead for them this weekend.

Try and remember how to act like a Luthor tonight, will you darling?”

A kiss like that would definitely fool a lot of people into thinking they were genuine. Kara figured she’d probably be fooled too if she didn’t know Lena was just acting.

    Chapter 2

The day had been a rush until that point, the drive, meeting Lillian, getting ready, kissing Lena. She was doing her best to not think about the last part and how kissing Lena had felt so different to any kiss she’d ever had before. Reasoning that it shouldn’t have surprised her that Lena would kiss like that. Even with Lena at her side she worried she’d be seen as an imposter and ruin the entire plan.

She’d be plotting to takeover my company in a heartbeat,” Lena’s eyes sparkled with mirth as Kara watched her closely, smiling back just as hard.
